# Progress

This file is the **only** place to track port progress. It is created and updated by agents during the port. `docs/` is read-only.

Generated from `loretta-rs/nodes.json` (`744` distinct symbols, `803` MemberDeclarationSyntax, `52` files) + `edges.json` (`1335` edges `declares`/`calls`/`type-uses`/`inherits`/`implements`/`overrides`/`contains-nested`) + `topo.json` (bottom-up). Stage 1-2 gates passed; Stage 2 topo: `402` leaves sorted, `342` in SCC appended file/line (withheld as clusters per `docs/PLAN.md`).

## How to update

- One row per graph node (type, method, property, field, enum, etc.) — not per file or per module.
- Status: `pending` / `claimed` / `done` — no `blocked`; revert and re-queue, or open a `spec:` PR if the spec is wrong
- Update this file in the same PR that lands the node.
- Nodes are bottom-up (topo.json): leaf types → utilities → dependents. Claim any `pending` node whose dependencies (see `edges.json` `From -> To`) are already `done`. SCC nodes (last 342) are withheld as clusters — stub shape first.
